# The Tide Mother — Video Prompts for Grok Imagine (cinematic blocking)

*171 beats, 31:59 total. Generate each clip at the length shown (6, 10 or 15 s), then trim it to the edit length. Each conversation opens on a high aerial view of everyone, then cuts in to close-ups. Only one named SPEAKER talks in each time window; everyone else stays silent.*

**How to use in Grok Imagine**
- Upload the matching image (Beat 108, or Shot 153A / 153B), choose **Make video**, set **16:9** and the clip length shown, and paste the prompt.
- For A/B beats, put the clips back to back in the edit: A, then B (then C if there is one).
- Keep each character's voice line identical across clips; see `06-characters-voices-behaviors.md`.
